##### Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: s8812.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "-0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}

  - id: s8812.h_taking_eic
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You are taking the EIC on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 27a"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [f1040.line27a, {const: 0}]}

  - id: s8812.h_ew1a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned Income Worksheet 1a. Enter the amount from line 1z of Form 1040, 1040-SR, or 1040-NR"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line1z}
    note: "Line 1b (nontaxable combat pay) is not computed: filers with combat pay are refused through s8812.combat_pay, so it is zero here. Lines 2a to 2e (statutory employee, Schedule C, Schedule F, K-1 amounts) are not computed: the catalog has no return-level Schedule C aggregate, so a self-employed filer is refused where this worksheet is used (eic.self_employed); statutory employee, farm and K-1 income are outside scope.schedule1."

  - id: s8812.h_ew_stop
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned Income Worksheet 3. If zero or less, stop. Do not complete the rest of this worksheet"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, le: [s8812.h_ew1a, {const: 0}]}

  - id: s8812.h_ew7_figured
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned Income Worksheet 7. Subtract line 6 (lines 4 and 5: Medicaid waiver payments excluded on Schedule 1 line 8s, and Schedule 1 line 15) from line 3"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: s8812.h_ew1a, take: s1.line15}
    note: "Line 4 (Medicaid waiver payments excluded on Schedule 1 line 8s) is outside scope (scope.other_income, scope.schedule1) and is taken as zero, so line 6 is Schedule 1 line 15."

  - id: s8812.h_ew7
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned Income Worksheet line 7 (or -0- if line 3 is zero or less)"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [s8812.h_ew_stop, {const: true}]}
          then: s8812.h_zero
        - guard: {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [s8812.h_ew_stop, {const: true}]}}
          then: s8812.h_ew7_figured

  - id: s8812.h_18a_refuse
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 18a needs an amount this tool does not figure (nontaxable combat pay, Schedule C income on the Earned Income Worksheet, or clergy housing/meals with Worksheet B)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [s8812.show_iia, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [s8812.combat_pay, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [s8812.h_taking_eic, {const: false}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [s8812.h_taking_eic, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.show_wsb4b, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.clergy, {const: true}]}

  - id: s8812.line18a
    printed: "18a"
    label: "Earned income (see instructions)"
    type: Dollars
    shown_when: s8812.show_iia
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [s8812.h_taking_eic, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [eic.show_wsb4b, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.wsb_4b
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [s8812.h_taking_eic, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [eic.show_wsb4b, {const: true}]}}
          then: eic.earned_income
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [s8812.h_taking_eic, {const: false}]}
          then: s8812.h_ew7
    refuses: [s8812.h_18a_refuse]
    note: "Earned Income Chart. Open points: (1) 'taking the EIC' is read as Form 1040 line 27a more than zero; (2) 'completed Worksheet B' is read as eic.show_wsb4b; (3) the optional-method row (Earned Income Worksheet even when taking the EIC) is not distinguished, because no catalog line identifies use of a Schedule SE optional method; (4) 'plus all of your nontaxable combat pay' is zero because combat pay is refused; the clergy subtraction is refused (eic.clergy). Shown only when Part II-A is reached (line 16a/16b not zero)."

  - id: s8812.h_combat_refuse
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Nontaxable combat pay received where Part II-A is completed"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [s8812.show_iia, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [s8812.combat_pay, {const: true}]}

  - id: s8812.line18b
    printed: "18b"
    label: "Nontaxable combat pay (see instructions)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    shown_when: s8812.show_iia
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
    refuses: [s8812.h_combat_refuse]
    note: "The catalog supplies only a Bool for combat pay and no amount; the tool computes only the case of no nontaxable combat pay (zero) and refuses otherwise."

  - id: s8812.h_clwb3_refuse
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Credit Limit Worksheet B line 3 needs an Earned Income Worksheet amount this tool does not figure (combat pay or Schedule C income)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [s8812.show_clwb, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [s8812.combat_pay, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}

  - id: s8812.clwb_3
    printed: "(CLW B 3)"
    label: "Enter your earned income from line 7 of the Earned Income Worksheet"
    type: Dollars
    shown_when: s8812.show_clwb
    expr: {op: copy, from: s8812.h_ew7}
    refuses: [s8812.h_clwb3_refuse]
    note: "Credit Limit Worksheet B is completed only when the conditions under Credit Limit Worksheet A line 3 are met (s8812.show_clwb); refusals apply only then."

Shown only when Part II-A is reached (line 16a/16b not zero).
